                                                                            Guotai Junan International | Annual Report 2022   113

                                                                                                       Report of the Directors




The Independent Non-executive Directors have reviewed and confirmed that the aforesaid continuing connected
transactions were entered into (i) in the ordinary and usual course of business of the Group; (ii) on normal commercial
terms or better; and (iii) in accordance with the relevant agreements governing them on terms that are fair and
reasonable and in the interests of the Shareholders as a whole.

According to the Group’s policy on pricing and internal control measures for continuing connected transactions,
the relevant business units are required to consult and obtain pre-approvals from the relevant departments for each
continuing connected transaction (except for brokerage transactions with standard brokerage rate applied). Each of
the legal and compliance, risk management, finance departments and office of the board of the Company is assigned
to review each of the continuing connected transactions from their respective expertise with reference to comparable
transactions with independent third parties. The Group regularly monitors transactions between the Group and the
GTJA Group as to whether such transactions fall within the scope of the Master Agreement, whether the relevant
annual caps have nearly been fully utilised, and issues reminders to the responsible business units where necessary.
The Group also conducts sampling inspections based on the internal audit plan so as to ensure the appropriateness
and effectiveness of its internal control procedures.

The Directors engaged the auditor of the Company to report on the aforesaid continuing connected transactions in
accordance with Hong Kong Standard on Assurance Engagement 3000 (Revised) “Assurance Engagements Other
Than Audits or Reviews of Historical Financial Information” (“HKSAE 3000”) and with reference to Practice Note 740
“Auditor’s letter on Continuing Connected Transactions under the Hong Kong Listing Rules” (“PN740”) issued by the
Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants.

The auditor has reported to the Directors in accordance with HKSAE 3000 and with reference to PN740 that the
transactions (i) had been approved by the Board; (ii) were in accordance with the pricing policies of the Company;
(iii) had been entered into in accordance with the relevant agreements governing such transactions; and (iv) had not
exceeded the relevant annual caps.

Disclosure Pursuant to Rule 13.21 of the Listing Rules
On 21 August 2020, the Company (as guarantor) and Guotai Junan (Hong Kong) limited (“GJHK”), a wholly-owned
subsidiary of the Company (as borrower), entered into several committed facility agreements with certain banks in Hong
Kong (as lenders) (the “1st Facility Agreement(s)”) in relation to the committed revolving facilities with an aggregate
amount of up to HK$2,150,000,000 with final repayment date falling 36 months after the date of respective 1st Facility
Agreement(s) and an aggregate amount of up to HK$1,350,000,000 with the final repayment date falling 24 months
after the date of respective 1st Facility Agreement(s).

On 25 September 2020, the Company (as guarantor) and GJHK (as borrower) entered into a committed facility
agreement with a bank in Hong Kong (as lender) (the “2nd Facility Agreement”) in relation to the committed revolving
facility with an amount of up to HK$500,000,000 with the final repayment date falling 24 months after the date of the
2nd Facility Agreement.

On 31 October 2022, the Company (as borrower) entered into several committed facility agreements with certain banks
in Hong Kong (as lenders) (the “3rd Facility Agreement(s)”, together with the 1st Facility Agreement(s) and the 2nd
Facility Agreement collectively referred to as the “Facility Agreements”) in relation to the committed revolving facilities
with an aggregate amount of up to HK$1,650,000,000 with final repayment date falling 36 months after the date of
respective 3rd Facility Agreement(s).
114   Guotai Junan International | Annual Report 2022



Report of the Directors




Under the Facility Agreements, it will be an event of default if Guotai Junan Securities Co., Ltd., the controlling
shareholder of the Company, is not, or ceases (directly or indirectly) to be the single largest legal and beneficial holder
of shares in the Company or does not, or cease to, control the Company. In case of the occurrence of an event of
default, the lender(s) may, by notice to borrower(s), (i) cancel all or part of the commitment(s); and/or (ii) declare all
or part of the loan(s), together with accrued interest, and all other amounts accrued or outstanding under the Facility
Agreements be immediately due and payable; and/or (iii) declare that all or part of the loan(s) become payable on
demand.

The committed revolving facilities with an aggregate amount of up to HK$1,350,000,000 under the 1st Facility
Agreement(s) were expired on 21 August 2022 and the 2nd Facility Agreement was expired on 25 September 2022.


Permitted Indemnity
Subject to the CO and under the Articles of Association, every Director is entitled to be indemnified out of the assets
of the Company against all losses and liabilities which he or she may sustain or incur in or about the execution or
discharge of his or her duties and/or the exercise of his or her powers and/or otherwise in connection with his or
her duties, powers or office. In addition, the Company has maintained appropriate Directors’ and Officers’ Liability
Insurance in respect of relevant legal actions against the directors and officers.


Sufficiency of Public Float
Based on information that is publicly available to the Company and within the knowledge of the Directors, at least 25%
of the total number of issued Shares was held by the public throughout the year ended 31 December 2022 and up to
the date of this report.

Compliance with Laws and Regulations
The major laws and regulations that have a significant impact on the Group’s business include the CO, the SFO, the
Anti-Money Laundering and Counter-Terrorist Financing Ordinance (Cap. 615 of the Laws of Hong Kong) and the
Listing Rules. Throughout the year of 2022 and as at the date of this report, as far as the Board and management are
aware, there was no material breach of, or non-compliance with, the applicable laws and regulations by the Group that
has a significant impact on its businesses and operations.

Environmental, Social and Corporate Responsibility
The Company creates sustainable value for its stakeholders by proactively managing risks deriving from environmental,
social and governance conditions. ESG practices are embedded across all operations of the Group and the Board
recognizes the different needs in economic, environmental and social by various stakeholder groups. In achieving
ESG-related goals, the Company has identified certain ESG areas that present challenges and opportunities for its
businesses to operate in a responsible and sustainable manner.

In 2022, the Company has adopted its climate change-related policy, commenced its climate risk assessment and
disclosed the related information with reference to the recommendations of the Task Force on Climate-related Financial
Disclosures (TCFD), aiming to tackle and respond to climate change-related concerns of various stakeholders of the
Group. For further details about the ESG performance of the Company, please refer to the 2022 ESG Report of the
Company.

Reporting Council Ordinance, who will retire, being eligible, and has offered itself for the re-appointment at the AGM to

 Expected credit loss assessment for loans and advances to customers

 Refer to Note 16 to the consolidated financial statements and the accounting policies on pages 145-181.

 The Key Audit Matter                                             How the matter was addressed in our audit
 關鍵審計事項                                                           該事項在我們的審計中是如何應對的

 Management assesses whether the credit risk of loans             •   understanding and assessing the appropriateness of
 and advances to customers and financial investments                  the established policies and procedures for the ECL
 have increased significantly since their initial recognition         assessment including the staging criteria, application
 and applies a three-stage impairment model to calculate              of assumptions and inputs into the ECL model;
 their ECL. A financial asset is classified as stage 1 when       •   了解及評估所制定的預期信用損失評估政策及程
 its credit risk has not increased significantly since its            序的合適度，包括階段標準、假設運用及預期信
 initial recognition; otherwise, it is classified as stage 2 or       用損失模型輸入數據；
 stage 3. Stage 3 applies when a financial asset is credit-
 impaired. The loss allowance for stage 1 financial assets
 is measured at an amount equal to a 12-month ECL. The
 loss allowance for stage 2 and stage 3 financial assets is
 measured at an amount equal to a lifetime ECL.
 管理層會評估給予客戶的貸款及墊款以及金融投資的信貸
 風險自初始確認以來是否顯著增加，採用三階段減值模型
 計算其預期信用損失。當信貸風險自初始確認以來並無顯
 著增加，則金融資產歸入第一階段，否則歸入第二或第三
 階段。第三階段適用於信貸減值的金融資產。第一階段金
 融資產的虧損撥備乃按相等於12個月預期信用損失的金額
 計量。第二及第三階段金融資產虧損撥備乃按相等於全期
 預期信用損失的金額計量。

2.1 Basis of preparation                                                          2.1 編製基準
      These financial statements have been prepared in accordance                   該等財務報表乃根據香港會計師公
      with Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”) (which                會（「香 港 會 計 師 公 會」）頒 佈 的 香
      include all applicable HKFRSs, Hong Kong Accounting Standards                 港財務報告準則（「香港財務報告準
      (“HKASs”) and Interpretations) issued by the Hong Kong Institute              則」）（其 包 括 所 有 適 用 的 香 港 財 務
      of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”), accounting principles             報 告 準 則、香 港 會 計 準 則（「香 港 會
      generally accepted in Hong Kong and the Hong Kong Companies                   計 準 則」）及 詮 釋）、香 港 公 認 會 計
      Ordinance. They have been prepared under the historical cost                  原則及香港《公司條例》編製。除按
      convention, except for financial assets and financial liabilities at fair     公平值計入損益的金融資產及金融
      value through profit or loss, financial assets at fair value through          負 債、按 公 平 值 計 入 其 他 全 面 收 益
      other comprehensive income, debt securities in issue designated at            的 金 融 資 產、指 定 按 公 平 值 計 入 損
      fair value through profit or loss and derivative financial instruments        益的已發行債務證券以及衍生金融
      which have been measured at fair value.                                       工 具 以 公 平 值 計 量 外，該 等 財 務 報
                                                                                    表已根據歷史成本慣例編製。

      These financial statements are presented in Hong Kong dollars                 除 非 另 有 說 明，該 等 財 務 報 表 乃 以
      and all values are rounded to the nearest thousand except when                港 元 呈 列，且 所 有 數 值 已 約 整 至 最
      otherwise stated.                                                             接近之千位數。

      Basis of consolidation                                                        綜合基準
      The consolidated financial statements include the financial                  綜合財務報表載有截至2022年12月
      statements of the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively                 31日止年度的本公司及其附屬公司
      referred to as the “Group”) for the year ended 31 December 2022.             （下 文 統 稱「本 集 團」）財 務 報 表。附
      A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity), directly or       屬公司為本公司直接或間接控制的
      indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control is achieved when              實體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團
      the Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its            承受或享有參與被投資公司業務的
      involvement with the investee and has the ability to affect those            可變回報以及能通過對被投資公司
      returns through its power over the investee (i.e., existing rights that      的 權 力（即 本 集 團 獲 賦 予 現 有 能 力
      give the Group the current ability to direct the relevant activities of      以主導被投資公司相關活動的既存
      the investee).                                                               權 利）影 響 該 等 回 報 時，即 取 得 控
                                                                                   制權。

2.1 Basis of preparation (continued)                                           2.1 編製基準（續）
     Basis of consolidation (continued)                                          綜合基準（續）
     When the Company has, directly or indirectly, less than a majority          倘本公司直接或間接擁有被投資公
     of the voting or similar rights of an investee, the Group considers all     司不足構成大多數的投票權或類似
     relevant facts and circumstances in assessing whether it has power          權 利，則 本 集 團 於 評 估 其 是 否 擁 有
     over an investee, including:                                                對被投資公司的權力時會考慮一切
                                                                                 相關事實及情況，包括：

     (a)    the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders of           (a)   與被投資公司其他投票權持
            the investee;                                                              有人的合約安排；

     (b)    rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and              (b)   其他合約安排所產生的權利；
                                                                                       及

     (c)    the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.               (c)   本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

     The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared for               附屬公司的財務報表乃與本公司於
     the same reporting period as the Company, using consistent                  相同申報期間採納一致的會計政策
     accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries are consolidated           編 製。附 屬 公 司 業 績 自 本 集 團 取 得
     from the date on which the Group obtains control, and continue to           控 制 權 當 日 起 綜 合 入 賬，並 將 一 直
     be consolidated until the date that such control ceases.                    綜合入賬直至該控制權終止當日。

     Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive income             損益及其他全面收益的各組成部分

     on consolidation.                                                           現金流量將於綜合入賬時悉數抵銷。

2.3 Summary of significant accounting policies                                   2.3 主要會計政策概要
     Fair value measurement                                                        公平值計量
     The Group measures its financial assets and financial liabilities at          本集團於各報告期末按公平值計量
     fair value through profit or loss, financial assets at fair value through     其按公平值計入損益的金融資產及
     other comprehensive income, debt securities in issue designated at            金 融 負 債、按 公 平 值 計 入 其 他 全 面
     fair value through profit or loss and derivative financial instruments,       收 益 的 金 融 資 產、指 定 為 按 公 平 值
     fund investments, debt investments and equity investments at fair             計 入 損 益 之 已 發 行 債 務 證 券、衍 生
     value at the end of each reporting period. Fair value is the price that       金 融 工 具、基 金 投 資、債 務 投 資 及
     would be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an      股 本 投 資。公 平 值 為 市 場 參 與 者 於
     orderly transaction between market participants at the measurement            計量日期在有序交易中出售資產所
     date. The fair value measurement is based on the presumption that             收取的價格或轉讓負債所支付的價
     the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place       格。公 平 值 計 量 乃 基 於 出 售 資 產 或
     either in the principal market for the asset or liability, (or in the         轉讓負債的交易於資產或負債主要
     absence of a principal market) in the most advantageous market for            市場或（在無主要市場情況下）最具
     the asset or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market         優勢的資產或負債市場進行的假設
     must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a              而 作 出。主 要 或 最 具 優 勢 市 場 須 為
     liability is measured using the assumptions that market participants          本 集 團 可 進 入 之 市 場。資 產 或 負 債
     would use when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that market           的公平值乃採用市場參與者於資產
     participants act in their economic best interest.                             或 負 債 定 價 時 會 採 用 的 假 設 計 量，
                                                                                   並假定市場參與者以其最佳經濟利
                                                                                   益行事。

     A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into                  非金融資產的公平值計量須計及市
     account a market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits          場參與者通過最大限度使用該資產
     by using the asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to            達 致 最 佳 用 途，或 將 該 資 產 出 售 予
     another market participant that would use the asset in its highest            將最大限度使用該資產達致最佳用
     and best use.                                                                 途 的 其 他 市 場 參 與 者，以 產 生 經 濟
                                                                                   效益的能力。

     The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in                   本集團採納適用於不同情況且具備
     the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to              充分數據以供計量公平值的估值方
     measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable inputs          法，以 盡 量 使 用 相 關 可 觀 察 參 數 及
     and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.                                盡量減少使用無法觀察參數。

                             directly or indirectly)                                          接）的估值方法

                             lowest level input that is significant to the fair               言屬重大的最低層參

      each reporting period.

      amount is determined for the cash-generating unit to which the                產 或 資 產 組 別 的 現 金 流 入，在 此 情

2.3 Summary of significant accounting policies                                2.3 主要會計政策概要（續）
     (continued)

     Leases                                                                     租賃
     The Group assesses at contract inception whether a contract is, or         本集團於合約開始時評估合約是否
     contains, a lease. A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract     屬 於 或 包 含 租 賃。倘 合 約 授 予 權 利
     conveys the right to control the use of an identified asset for a          以代價為交換在某一時期內控制使
     period of time in exchange for consideration.                              用 已 識 別 資 產，則 合 約 屬 於 或 包 含
                                                                                租約。

     Group as a lessee                                                          本集團作為承租人
     The Group applies a single recognition and measurement approach            本集團就所有租賃應用單一確認及
     for all leases, except for short-term leases and leases of low-value       計 量 方 法，惟 短 期 租 賃 及 低 價 值 資
     assets. The Group recognises lease liabilities to make lease               產 租 賃 除 外。本 集 團 確 認 作 出 租 賃
     payments and right-of-use assets representing the right to use the         付 款 的 租 賃 負 債 及 使 用 權 資 產（即
     underlying assets.                                                         使用相關資產的權利）。

     (a)   Right-of-use assets                                                  (a)   使用權資產
           Right-of-use assets are recognised at the commencement                     使用權資產乃於租賃開始日
           date of the lease (that is the date the underlying asset is                期（即相關資產可供使用日期）
           available for use). Right-of-use assets are measured at cost,              確認。使用權資產按成本減任
           less any accumulated depreciation and any impairment                       何累計折舊及任何減值虧損
           losses, and adjusted for any remeasurement of lease liabilities.           計量，並就任何重新計量租賃
           The cost of right-of-use assets includes the amount of lease               負債作出調整。使用權資產的

            gain or loss.                                                                  相關差額確認為收益或虧損。

            (ii) ECL allowance                                                         (ii) 預期信用損失撥備
                  The Group applies a simplified approach to measure ECL                 本集團應用簡易方法計
                  on trade receivable and a general approach to measure                  量交易應收款項的預期
                  ECL on loans and advances to customers, time deposits                  信 用 損 失，並 應 用 一 般
                  and other financial assets accounted for at amortised                  方法計量給予客戶的貸
                  cost as well as loan commitment.                                       款 及 墊 款、定 期 存 款 及
                                                                                         其他按攤銷成本入賬的
                                                                                         金融資產以及貸款承擔
                                                                                         的預期信用損失。

                  Under the simplified approach, the Group measures the                  根 據 簡 易 方 法，本 集 團
                  loss based on lifetime ECL. Under the general approach,                根據全期預期信用損失
                  financial assets migrate through the following three                   計 量 虧 損。按 照 一 般 方
                  stages based on the change in credit risk since initial                法，金 融 工 具 乃 基 於 初
                  recognition:                                                           步確認後的信貸風險變
                                                                                         動，通 過 下 列 三 個 階 段
                                                                                         予以轉撥：

                  Stage 1: 12-month ECL                                                  第 一 階 段：12個 月 的
                                                                                         預期信用損失
                  For exposures where there has not been a significant                   就自初步確認後的信貸
                  increase in credit risk since initial recognition and that             風 險 並 無 重 大 變 動，且
                  are not credit-impaired upon origination, the portion                  於產生時並無信貸減值
                  of the lifetime ECL associated with the probability of                 的 情 況 而 言，乃 將 與 未
                  default events occurring within the next 12 months is                  來十二個月出現違約事
                  recognised.                                                            件的可能性有關的全期
                                                                                         預期信用損失部分予以
                                                                                         確認。

                  Stage 2: Lifetime ECL – not credit-impaired                            第 二 階 段：全 期 預 期
                                                                                         信 用 損 失－並 無 信 貸
                                                                                         減值

     (continued)

     Derivative financial instruments                                            衍生金融工具
     The Group’s derivative financial instruments are initially recognised       本集團衍生金融工具於衍生合約訂
     at their fair value on the date on which a derivative contract is           立 日 期 初 步 按 其 公 平 值 確 認，其 後
     entered into and are subsequently remeasured at fair value.                 按公平值再次計量。

     Derivatives are carried as assets when the fair value is positive and       倘 公 平 值 為 正 數，衍 生 工 具 以 資 產
     as liabilities when the fair value is negative. Any gains or losses         入 賬，而 公 平 值 倘 為 負 數，則 以 負
     arising from changes in fair value of derivatives are taken directly to     債 入 賬。衍 生 工 具 的 公 平 值 變 動 產
     statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income.                 生的任何盈虧直接計入損益及其他
                                                                                 全面收益表。

     Whilst the Group enters into a derivative contract for trading              當本集團根據本集團風險管理框架
     purposes or to provide economic hedges under the Group’s risk               為交易目的或提供經濟對沖而訂立
     management framework, it does not apply hedge accounting.                   衍 生 工 具 合 約 時，並 不 應 用 對 沖 會
                                                                                 計。

     Offsetting of financial instruments                                         抵銷金融工具
     Financial assets and financial liabilities are offset and the net           倘存在現時可強制執行的法律權利
     amount is reported in the statement of financial position if there is       可 抵 銷 已 確 認 金 額，且 有 意 以 淨 額
     a currently enforceable legal right to offset the recognised amounts        基準結算或同時變現資產及清償
     and there is an intention to settle on a net basis, or to realise the       負 債，則 可 抵 銷 金 融 資 產 及 金 融 負
     assets and settle the liabilities simultaneously.                           債，而淨額於財務狀況表呈報。

     Shares held under the share award scheme                                    股份獎勵計劃項下持有的股
                                                                                 份
     Own equity instruments which are reacquired (shares held under              購 回 本 身 的 權 益 工 具（股 份 獎 勵 計
     the share award scheme) are recognised at cost and deducted                 劃 項 下 持 有 的 股 份）按 成 本 確 認 並
     from equity. No gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss on the         於 權 益 中 扣 除。本 集 團 購 買、出
     purchase, sale, issue or cancellation of the Group’s own equity             售、發 行 或 註 銷 本 身 的 權 益 工 具 所
     instruments. Any difference between the carrying amount and the             得 收 益 或 虧 損 概 不 於 損 益 賬 確 認。
     consideration is recognised in equity.                                      賬面值與代價之間的任何差額於權
                                                                                 益確認。

      Client trust bank balances                                                    客戶信託銀行結餘
      The Group maintains trust and segregated accounts with authorised            本集團於獲授權金融機構開設信託
      financial institutions to hold clients’ deposits arising from normal         及 獨 立 賬 戶，以 保 管 客 戶 來 自 一 般
      business transactions. The Group has classified the clients’ monies          業 務 交 易 的 存 款。本 集 團 將 客 戶 款
      as client trust bank balances under the current assets in the                項歸類為綜合財務狀況表流動資產

      and recognised corresponding accounts payable to the respective              全 部 利 息 收 入，並 基 於 其 對 客 戶 款
      customers in the current liabilities clients on grounds that it is liable    項的任何損失或挪用負責而確認應
      for any loss or misappropriation of clients’ monies. Under the Hong          付流動負債賬戶內有關客戶的相應
      Kong Securities and Futures Ordinance (Cap. 571) and the Hong                款 項。根 據 香 港 法 例 第571章 香 港
      Kong Insurance Companies Ordinance (Cap. 41), the Group is not               《證券及期貨條例》及香港法例第41
      allowed to use the clients’ monies to settle its own obligation.             章香港《保險公司條例》，本集團不

      statements as the Group has no contractual rights to these assets             何收益或虧損不會計入該等財務報
      and its gains or losses under fiduciary activities.                           表，原 因 為 本 集 團 對 該 等 資 產 及 其

     Provisions                                                                 撥備
     A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal or              由於過往發生的事件引致目前出現
     constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and it is             法 定 或 推 定 責 任，而 該 等 責 任 很 可
     probable that a future outflow of resources will be required to settle     能 導 致 日 後 資 源 流 出 以 履 行 責 任，
     the obligation, provided that a reliable estimate can be made of the       並 能 夠 可 靠 估 計 責 任 金 額 時，則 確
     amount of the obligation.                                                  認撥備。

     When the effect of discounting is material, the amount recognised          當 有 重 大 貼 現 影 響 時，會 就 預 期 須
     for a provision is the present value at the end of the reporting           用作支付責任的未來開支於報告期
     period of the future expenditures expected to be required to settle        未 的 現 值 確 認 撥 備。因 時 間 流 逝 所
     the obligation. The increase in the discounted present value amount        導 致 折 現 現 值 的 金 額 增 加，會 列 入
     arising from the passage of time is included in finance costs in           損益賬的融資成本。
     profit or loss.

     to items recognised outside profit or loss is recognised outside           益 賬 外 確 認 的 各 項 目，其 有 關 所 得
     profit or loss, either in other comprehensive income or directly in        稅於損益賬外的其他全面收益或直
     equity.                                                                    接於權益內確認。

     are measured at the amount expected to be recovered from or                負債乃經考慮本集團經營所在國家

     the reporting period, taking into consideration interpretations and        稅 務 法 例），按 預 期 可 自 稅 務 機 關

   estimates (continued)
     Estimation uncertainty (continued)                                         估計不確定因素（續）
     Valuation of financial assets at fair value through profit or             按公平值計入損益及按公平值
     loss and fair value through other comprehensive income                    計入其他全面收益的金融資產
                                                                               估值
     The fair value of financial assets designated at fair value through        並非於活躍市場買賣的按公平值計
     profit or loss that are not traded in an active market is determined       入損益的金融資產的公平值採用外
     by using external valuations or valuation techniques. The Group            部 估 值 或 估 值 方 法 釐 定。本 集 團 採
     uses a variety of methods and makes assumptions that are mainly            用多種方法及作出多項主要以各報
     based on market conditions existing at each reporting date.                告 日 期 市 況 為 基 準 的 假 設。所 用 估
     Valuation techniques used include the use of comparable recent             值方法包括使用可資比較近期公平
     arm’s length transactions, discounted cash flows analysis, option          磋 商 交 易、貼 現 現 金 流 量 分 析、期
     pricing models and other valuation techniques commonly used by             權定價模式及其他市場參與者普遍
     other market participants. Changes in assumptions on the valuation         使 用 的 其 他 估 值 方 式。有 關 估 值 方
     techniques could affect the reported fair values of these financial        法的假設變動可能影響該等金融資
     assets. Further details are contained in notes 20 and 40 to the            產 的 報 告 公 平 值。進 一 步 詳 情 載 於
     financial statements.                                                      財務報表附註20及40。

     operating businesses are structured and managed separately                 的經營業務乃按其營運性質及所提
     according to the nature of their operations and the services they          供 服 務 分 開 組 織 及 管 理。本 集 團 旗
     provide. Each of the Group’s operating segments represents a               下各經營分部均為提供服務的策略
     strategic business unit that offers services which are subject to          業 務 單 位，其 服 務 所 承 受 風 險 及 所
     risks and returns that are different from those of the other operating     獲回報有別於其他經營分部。
     segments.

     Loans and advances are categorised into “excellent”, “good”, “fair”       貸 款 及 墊 款 分 類 為「 優 秀 」、「 良
     and “individually impaired”. Excellent refers to exposures which          好」、「普 通」及「個 別 減 值」。優 秀
     margin obligation are consistently met and the payment of principal       指貫徹履行孖展責任及本金和利息
     and interest is not in doubt, collaterals provided are highly liquid      付 款 並 無 呆 賬，且 提 供 的 抵 押 品 具
     and of good quality. Good refers to exposures which principal             有 高 流 通 性 及 良 好 品 質 的 風 險。良
     and interest are fully secured but collaterals provided are at a          好指本金和利息全部有抵押但提供
     relatively lower quality where shortfall may be recorded when there       的 抵 押 品 質 量 相 對 較 低，且 抵 押 品
     is a significant drop in collateral value. Fair refers to exposures       價值大幅下跌時可能錄得缺額的風
     which principal and interest are fully secured but the quality of         險。普 通 指 本 金 和 利 息 全 部 有 抵 押
     the collateral is deteriorating, shortfall would be expected when         但 抵 押 品 質 量 正 在 變 差，且 抵 押 品
     the collateral value continues to deteriorate. Individually impaired      價值持續下跌時預期錄得缺額的風
     refers to exposures which loss, partial or full, has incurred and with    險。個 別 減 值 指 已 產 生 部 分 或 悉 數
     insufficient collateral.                                                  虧損且無足夠抵押品的風險。

16. Loans and advances to customers (continued)                              16. 給 予 客 戶 的 貸 款 及 墊 款
                                                                               （續）

     Margin financing operations                                                孖展融資業務
     The Group provides customers with margin financing for securities          本集團就證券交易向客戶提供孖展
     transactions, which are secured by customers’ securities held as           融 資，以 客 戶 的 證 券 持 作 抵 押 品 擔
     collateral. The maximum credit limit granted for each customer is          保。每 名 客 戶 獲 授 的 最 高 信 貸 額 度
     based on the customer’s financial background and the quality of            乃以客戶的財務背景及所持相關抵
     related collateral. The Group seeks to maintain strict control over        押 品 的 質 素 為 基 準。本 集 團 擬 保 持
     its outstanding receivables and has a credit and risk management           對 其 未 清 償 應 收 款 項 的 嚴 格 控 制，
     department to monitor credit risks.                                        並成立了信貸及風險管理部門監控
                                                                                信貸風險。

     Margin loans to the customers are secured by the underlying                給予客戶的孖展貸款以相關已抵押
     pledged securities, bear interest at a rate with reference to the          證 券 擔 保，按 根 據 港 元 最 優 惠 利 率
     Hong Kong dollar prime rate and are repayable on demand. The               釐 定 的 利 率 計 息，並 按 要 求 償 還。
     carrying value of margin loans approximates to their fair value. No        孖展貸款的賬面值與其公平值相
     ageing analysis is disclosed as, in the opinion of the directors, an       若。由 於 董 事 認 為，鑒 於 孖 展 貸 款
     ageing analysis does not give additional value in view of the nature       業 務 的 性 質，賬 齡 分 析 並 不 會 提 供
     of the margin loans business. The amount of credit facilities granted      額 外 價 值，故 概 無 披 露 賬 齡 分 析。
     to margin clients is determined by the discounted market value of          授予孖展客戶的信貸融資額乃根據
     the collateral securities accepted by the Group. As at 31 December         本集團接納的抵押證券貼現市值釐
     2022, the total value of securities pledged as collateral in respect       定。於2022年12月31日，作 為 孖 展
     of the margin loans was approximately HK$30,561 million (2021:             貸款抵押品的已抵押證券總值約為
     HK$58,946 million) based on the market value of the securities as          30,561百 萬 港 元（ 2021年：58,946
     at the end of the reporting period.                                        百 萬 港 元），此 乃 按 證 券 於 報 告 期
                                                                                末的市值計算得出。

     Term loans to customers                                                    給予客戶的定期貸款
     The Group also provides term loans to customers. In determining            本 集 團 亦 向 客 戶 提 供 定 期 貸 款。於
     the interest rates, reference is made to the credit standing of            釐 定 利 率 時，會 參 考 相 關 客 戶 的 財
     the relevant customers and the quality and value of the collateral         務信貸狀況以及所質押的抵押品質
     pledged. The term loans granted to customers amounted to                   素 及 價 值。於2022年12月31日 授 予
     HK$434,122,000 (2021: HK$1,210,522,000), and the accrued                   客 戶 的 定 期 貸 款 為 數434,122,000
     interest amounted to HK$33,540,000 (2021: HK$34,621,000) as                港 元（ 2021年：1,210,522,000港
     at 31 December 2022. The carrying amounts of the term loans to             元），其 應 計 利 息 為 數33,540,000
     customers approximate to their fair values.                                港 元（2021年：34,621,000港 元）。
                                                                                給予客戶的定期貸款的賬面值與其
                                                                                公平值相若。

      notional amounts. The notional amount, recorded gross, is the                    額。所 記 錄 的 名 義 金 額 總 額 為 衍 生
      amount of a derivative’s underlying asset, reference rate or index               工 具 相 關 資 產、參 考 匯 率 或 指 數 的
      and is the basis upon which changes in the value of the derivative               金 額，並 為 衍 生 工 具 價 值 計 量 變 動
      are measured. The notional amounts indicate the volume of                        的 基 準。名 義 金 額 表 明 於 年 末 未 完
      transactions outstanding at the year end and are indicative of                   成 的 交 易 量，既 不 是 市 場 風 險 亦 非
      neither the market risk nor the credit risk.                                     信貸風險的指標。

      of financial position as the counterparty retains substantially              財 務 狀 況 表 中 確 認，原 因 是 交 易 方
      all risks and returns of the securities. The amount paid by the              保留該等證券的絕大部分風險及回
      Group is recognised as receivable. In the event of default by                報。本 集 團 支 付 的 款 項 確 認 為 應 收
      the counterparty, the Group has the right to sell the underlying             款 項。倘 交 易 方 違 約，本 集 團 有 權
      securities for settling the outstanding receivable.                          出售相關證券以結算未結清的應收
                                                                                   款項。

      by the Group is recognised as liabilities as the Group retains                                  的 款 項 確 認 為 負 債，原 因 是 本 集 團
      substantially all risks and returns of the securities.                                          保留證券的絕大部分風險及回報。

32. Share award reserve                                                   32. 股份獎勵儲備
     On 27 October 2011, the Company adopted the share award                於2011年10月27日，本 公 司 採 納
     scheme (the “Share Award Scheme”) under which shares of the            股份獎勵計劃（「股份獎勵計劃」），
     Company (the “Awarded Shares”) may be awarded to selected              據 此，本 公 司 股 份（「獎 勵 股 份」）可
     employees (including directors) of any members of the Group            根據股份獎勵計劃的計劃規則及信
     (the “Selected Employees”) pursuant to the terms of the scheme         託契據條款授予本集團任何成員公
     rules and trust deed of the Share Award Scheme. The share              司的獲選僱員（包括董事）       （「獲選僱
     award scheme became effective on the adoption date and, unless         員」）。股 份 獎 勵 計 劃 於 採 納 日 期 生
     otherwise terminated or amended, will remain in force for 10 years     效，除 非 另 行 終 止 或 修 訂，否 則 將
     from that date, i.e., 26 October 2021.                                 自 該 日 期 起 十 年（即 至2021年10月
                                                                            26日）有效。

     The aggregate number of the Awarded Shares permitted to be            於股份獎勵計劃期間於股份獎勵計
     awarded under the Share Award Scheme throughout the duration          劃項下獲准授出的獎勵股份總數不
     of the Share Award Scheme is limited to 10% of the issued share       得超過本公司於採納日期已發行股
     capital of the Company as at the adoption date (i.e. 164,000,000      本的10%（即164,000,000股股份）。
     shares). The maximum number of the Awarded Shares which may           向獲選僱員授出的獎勵股份最高數
     be awarded to a Selected Employee in any 12-month period up to        目在截至授出股份之日（包含此日）
     and including the date of award shall not in aggregate exceed 1%      止任何十二個月期間合共不得超逾
     of the issued share capital of the Company as at the adoption date    本公司於採納日期已發行股本的1%
     (i.e. 16,400,000 shares).                                             （即16,400,000股股份）。

     When a Selected Employee has satisfied all vesting conditions,        倘獲選僱員符合本公司董事會於作
     which might include service and/or performance conditions,            出有關獎勵時列明的全部歸屬條件
     specified by the board of directors of the Company at the time        （其 中 可 能 包 括 服 務 年 期 及 ╱ 或 表
     of making the award and become entitled to the shares of the          現 的 條 件），便 可 獲 取 有 關 獎 勵 的
     Company forming the subject of the award, the trustee shall           本 公 司 股 份，受 託 人 須 無 償 向 該 名
     transfer the relevant Awarded Shares to that employee at no cost.     僱員轉讓有關獎勵股份。

     The voting rights and powers of any shares held under the Share        獨立受託人有權行使股份獎勵計劃
     Award Scheme shall be exercised by the independent trustee who         項下持有的任何股份的投票權及權
     shall abstain from voting.                                             力，惟獨立受託人將放棄投票。

37. Unconsolidated structured entity                                          37. 未綜合結構性實體
      A structured entity is an entity in which voting rights are not the       結構性實體為投票權並非決定控制
      dominant factor in deciding control. Structured entity is generally       權 的 主 導 因 素 的 實 體。結 構 性 實 體
      created to achieve a narrow and well defined objective with               通常為實現有限而明確的目標而
      restrictions around their ongoing activities.                             設，其持續經營活動受到限制。

      Depending on the Group’s power over the activities of the entity          視乎本集團對實體活動的權力及其
      and its exposure to and the ability to influence its own returns, it      受實體回報的影響程度及其影響實
      may consolidate the entity. In other cases, it may sponsor or have        體 回 報 的 能 力 而 定，其 可 能 將 實 體
      an interest in such an entity but does not consolidate it. The Group      綜 合 入 賬。在 其 他 情 況 下，其 可 能
      sponsors an entity when:                                                  資 助 實 體 或 於 實 體 中 擁 有 權 益，但
                                                                                不 將 實 體 綜 合 入 賬。下 列 情 況 屬 本
                                                                                集團贊助一間實體：

      (i)     It is the majority user of the entity;                            (i)     其為該實體的主要用戶；

      (ii)    Its name appears in the name of the entity or on the products     (ii)    其名稱出現在該實體的名稱
              issued by the entity;                                                     或該實體發行的產品上；

      (iii)   It provides implicit or explicit guarantees of the entity’s       (iii)   其為該實體的業績提供隱含
              performance; and                                                          或明確的擔保；

      (iv)    It led the formation of the entity.                               (iv)    其牽頭成立該實體。

      The unconsolidated structured entities invest in a range of asset         未綜合結構性實體投資於多種資產
      classes. The Group receives income from these unconsolidated              類 別。本 集 團 通 過 自 該 等 實 體 發 行
      structured entities through the receipt of arrangement fees and           的計息票據收取安排費用及利息收
      interest income from interest bearing notes issued by these entities.     入而自該等未綜合結構性實體收取
      As of 31 December 2022, the Group held notes of HK$2,359 million          收 入。截 至2022年12月31日，本 集
      (2021: HK$2,519 million), under financial assets held for trading and     團 在 持 作 買 賣 及 投 資 金 融 資 產（附
      investments (note 20), issued by these entities.                          註20 ）項 下 持 有 該 等 實 體 發 行 的 票
                                                                                據2,359百 萬 港 元（ 2021年：2,519
                                                                                百萬港元）。

40. Financial risk management                                                  40. 金融風險管理
     The Group’s activities mainly expose to financial risks such as             本 集 團 的 業 務 主 要 面 臨 信 貸 風 險、
     credit risk, market risk and liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk       市場風險及流動資金風險等金融
     management framework focuses on the analysis, evaluation,                   風 險。本 集 團 整 體 風 險 管 理 框 架 集
     acceptance and monitoring of these risks which are inevitable               中 於 分 析、評 估、接 受 及 監 控 該 等
     consequences of being in business. The Group’s aim is therefore             於 業 務 過 程 中 不 可 避 免 的 風 險。因
     to achieve an appropriate balance between risk and return and               此，本 集 團 的 目 標 為 適 當 地 平 衡 風
     minimise any potential adverse effect on the Group’s financial              險 與 回 報，並 將 對 本 集 團 財 務 表 現
     performance.                                                                潛在的任何不良影響減至最低。

     The Group’s risk management policies are approved by the board              本集團的風險管理政策由董事會批
     of directors and are designed to identify and analyse these risks, to       准，旨 在 識 別 及 分 析 上 述 風 險，設
     set appropriate risk limits and controls and to monitor the risks and       定適當的風險限額並控制及監控風
     adherence to limits by means of reliable and up-to-date information.        險及使用可靠及先進的資訊以嚴守
     The board of directors provides written principles for overall risk         限 額。董 事 會 就 整 體 風 險 管 理 制 定
     management. The Group regularly reviews its risk management                 書 面 原 則。本 集 團 定 期 檢 討 其 風 險
     policies to reflect any change in the market and clients.                   管 理 政 策，以 反 映 市 場 及 客 戶 的 任
                                                                                 何變動。

     The main financial risks arising from the Group’s financial                 本集團金融工具所產生的主要金融
     instruments are credit risk, market risk and liquidity risk. The            風 險 為 信 貸 風 險、市 場 風 險 及 流 動
     policies for managing each of these risks are summarised below:             資 金 風 險。管 理 各 項 風 險 的 政 策 概
                                                                                 括如下：

     Credit risk                                                                 信貸風險
     Credit exposures arise principally from loans and advances to               信貸風險主要源自本集團資產組合
     customers, accounts receivable, debt securities, bank balances and          中 的 給 予 客 戶 的 貸 款 及 墊 款、應 收
     client trust bank balances which are included in the Group’s asset          款 項、債 務 證 券、銀 行 結 餘 及 客 戶
     portfolio.                                                                  信託銀行結餘。

      Market risk                                                                 市場風險
      The Group takes on exposure to market risk, which is the risk               本 集 團 所 面 對 的 市 場 風 險，乃 指 由
      that the fair value or future cash flows of a financial instrument will     於市場價格變動而引致金融工具的
      fluctuate because of changes in market prices. Market risks mainly          公平值或未來現金流量變動的風
      arise from open positions in interest rate, currency and equity             險。市 場 風 險 乃 主 要 因 利 率、貨 幣
      products, all of which are exposed to general and specific market           及 股 權 產 品 等 的 未 平 倉 而 產 生，並
      movements and changes in the level of volatility of market rates or         受到一般及特定的市場變動及市場
      prices such as interest rates, foreign exchange rates and equity            利 率 或 價 格（如 利 率、匯 率 及 股 價）
      prices.                                                                     波幅變動的風險所影響。

      The financial instruments held by the Group mainly exposed                  本集團所持有的金融工具主要面對
      it to interest rate risk and price risk. The market risk mainly             利 率 風 險 及 價 格 風 險。市 場 風 險 主
      includes cash flow interest rate risk and price risk. The Group has         要包括現金流量利率風險及價格
      established policies and procedures for monitoring and controlling          風 險。本 集 團 已 制 定 有 關 政 策 及 程
      the market risk arising from these financial instruments.                   序，以 監 測 及 控 制 該 等 金 融 工 具 所
                                                                                  產生的市場風險。

     investments usually bear a fixed rate interest and they are managed       及 現 金 與 銀 行 結 餘 有 關。債 務 投 資

     interest-bearing with short term maturities within one year, of which     其他金融資產及負債乃不計息或按

     Price risk                                                                   價格風險
     The Group is exposed to equity securities price risk from equity             本集團因持有權益類衍生工具及基
     derivative instruments and fund investments held by the Group                金 投 資（於 財 務 狀 況 表 分 類 為 按 公
     which are classified in the statement of financial position as               平 值 計 入 損 益 的 金 融 資 產 及 負 債）
     financial assets and liabilities at fair value through profit or loss.       而 承 受 股 本 證 券 價 格 風 險。價 格 風
     Price risk is the risk of changes in fair value of financial instruments     險指金融工具公平值因波動而出現
     from fluctuations, whether such a change in price is caused by               變 動 的 風 險，無 論 有 關 價 格 變 動 是
     factors specific to the individual instrument or factors affecting all       因個別工具特有的因素或影響市場
     instruments traded in the markets. The Group mitigates its price             上 所 有 買 賣 工 具 的 因 素 所 引 致。本
     risk by performing detailed due diligence analysis of investments            集團通過對投資進行詳細的盡職分
     and dedicated professionals are assigned to oversee and monitor              析 降 低 價 格 風 險，並 指 派 專 業 人 士
     the performance of investments.                                              監督及監控投資表現。

     The sensitivity analysis below has been determined based on the              以下敏感度分析乃假設所有權益類
     assumption that the price of all the investment of equity instruments        產品及基金投資的價格根據與香港
     and funds move according to the historical correlation with the              恆生指數及其他相關股票指數之過
     Hong Kong Hang Seng Index and other relevant equity indices.                 往 關 係 變 動 而 釐 定。分 析 乃 以 假 設
     The analysis is based on the assumption that the equity index had            股 票 指 數 變 動10%而 其 他 變 數 維 持
     changed by 10% with all other variable held constant, it represents          不 變，這 代 表 管 理 層 對 股 票 指 數 合
     management’s assessment of the reasonably possible change in                 理可能變動的評估。
     equity index.

      Foreign currency risk                                                      外匯風險
      Foreign currency risk refers to the risk that movements in foreign         外匯風險指影響本集團財務業績及
      currency exchange rates will affect the Group’s financial results and      其現金流量的外幣匯率變動的風險。
      its cash flows.

      The Group’s foreign exchange risk principally arises from its              本集團的外匯風險主要來自其槓桿
      leveraged foreign exchange dealing and broking business as well as         式外匯買賣及經紀業務以及本集團
      the Group’s transactions which are denominated in currencies other         以港元以外貨幣為單位的交易。
      than the Hong Kong dollars.

      For the leveraged foreign exchange brokerage business, the Group           就 槓 桿 外 匯 經 紀 業 務 而 言，本 集 團
      hedges the majority of the client positions through back-to-back           通過與外部對手方的背對背交易對
      transactions with external counterparties. The Group adopts                沖 大 多 數 客 戶 的 倉 位。本 集 團 對 其
      stringent control over its positions to minimize its exposure to           倉 位 採 納 嚴 格 監 控，以 最 小 化 其 外
      foreign exchange risk.                                                     匯風險。

      The Group’s principal operations are transacted and recorded in            本 集 團 的 主 要 業 務 以 港 元、美 元
      Hong Kong dollars, United States dollars (“US$”) and Renminbi             （「美元」）及人民幣（「人民幣」）交易
      (“RMB”). The Group is not exposed to material foreign exchange             及 列 賬。由 於 港 元 與 美 元 掛 鈎，故
      risk because HK$ is pegged with US$. The Group also conducts               本 集 團 並 無 面 臨 重 大 外 匯 風 險。本
      appropriate hedging activities when it is exposed to material              集團亦於其面臨重大的人民幣外匯
      exchange risk in RMB. Other foreign currency exposure is relatively        風 險 時 採 取 適 當 的 對 沖 活 動。其 他
      minimal to its total assets and liabilities. The foreign exchange          外幣風險相對於其總資產及負債所
      risk is daily managed and monitored by the treasury and finance            承 接 者 相 對 較 低。外 匯 風 險 由 庫 務
      department.                                                                及財務部每日管理及監督。

     Liquidity risk                                                                流動資金風險
     Prudent liquidity risk management includes maintaining sufficient             審慎的流動資金風險管理包括維持
     cash, the availability of funding from an adequate amount of                  充 足 的 現 金，通 過 已 承 諾 信 貸 融 資
     committed credit facilities and the ability to close out market               的 足 夠 額 度 備 有 資 金，及 有 能 力 減
     positions. Due to the dynamic nature of the underlying business, the          少 市 場 持 倉。由 於 相 關 業 務 的 多 變
     treasury department maintains flexibility in funding by maintaining           性 質，庫 務 部 致 力 通 過 已 承 諾 的 可
     available committed credit facilities from the banks.                         用銀行信貸融資維持資金的靈活性。

     Sources of liquidity are daily reviewed by treasury department                庫 務 部 每 日 審 計 流 動 資 金 來 源，以
     to ensure the availability of sufficient liquid funds to meet all             確 保 可 獲 得 充 足 流 動 資 金，履 行 所
     obligations.                                                                  有責任。

     Management monitors rolling forecasts of the Group’s liquidity                管理層根據預期現金流量監控本集
     reserve (comprising undrawn borrowing facilities) and cash and cash           團 的 流 動 資 金 儲 備（包 括 未 提 取 借
     equivalents on the basis of expected cash flows in strict compliance          款 融 資）及 現 金 及 現 金 等 價 物 的 滾
     with statutory requirements. This is achieved by monitoring the               存 預 測，以 嚴 格 遵 守 法 定 要 求。為
     liquidity position of the individual companies within the Group on a          達 到 此 目 的，本 集 團 需 要 每 日 監 控
     daily basis to ensure the availability of sufficient liquid funds to meet     旗 下 公 司 的 流 動 資 金 狀 況，以 確 保
     all obligations and compliance with the statutory requirements such           能夠取得充足的流動資金履行所有
     as the Hong Kong Securities and Futures (Financial Resources)                 責 任，並 符 合 有 關 法 定 要 求（如 香
     Rules.                                                                        港證券及期貨（財政資源）規則）。

     Financial instruments measured at fair value (continued)                    按公平值計量的金融工具（續）
     Fair value hierarchy (continued)                                            公平值等級（續）
     (a) Financial instruments in Level 1                                        (a) 第1級金融工具
           The fair values of financial instruments traded in active                   於活躍市場買賣的金融工具
           markets are based on quoted market prices at the end of                     公平值根據報告期末的市場
           the reporting period. A market is regarded as active if quoted              報價計算。活躍的市場指可即
           prices are readily and regularly available from an exchange,                時及定期從交易所、交易商、
           dealer, or broker and those prices represent actual and                     或經紀取得報價的市場，而該
           regularly occurring market transactions on an arm’s length                  等報價反映按公平基準進行
           basis. The quoted market prices used for financial assets and               的實際及定期發生的市場交
           financial liabilities held by the Group are the current bid price           易。本集團所持金融資產及金
           and the current ask price.                                                  融負債所用的市場報價為當
                                                                                       時的買盤價及賣盤價。

     (b)   Financial instruments in Level 2                                      (b)   第2級金融工具
           The fair values of financial instruments that are not traded                並 非 於 活 躍 市 場（例 如 場 外 市
           in an active market (for example, over-the-counter) are                     場）買 賣 的 金 融 工 具 公 平 值 乃
           determined by using valuation techniques. These valuation                   使用估值技術釐定。該等估值
           techniques maximise the use of observable market data where                 技術盡可能採用可觀察市場
           it is available and rely as little as possible on entity specific           數 據（如 有），盡 量 不 依 賴 實
           estimates. If all significant inputs used in the valuation for              體的特定估計。如金融工具估
           financial instruments are observable, the financial instruments             值所用的所有重大參數均為
           are included in Level 2.                                                    可觀察參數，該金融工具列入
                                                                                       第2級。

                                                                                       倘有一個或多個重大參數並
           If one or more of the significant inputs are not based on                   非以可觀察市場數據為基準，
           observable market data, the financial instrument is included in             該金融工具列入第3級。
           Level 3.

     Capital management                                                      資本管理
     The Group’s objectives for managing capital, which represent a          本集團資本管理的目標概念比財務
     broader concept than the “equity” on the face of the statement of       狀況表的「權益」更為廣闊，即：
     financial position, are:

     •    to comply with the capital requirements under the Hong Kong        •   就本身為持牌法團的附屬公
          Securities and Futures (Financial Resources) Rules for its             司而言，符合香港證券及期貨
          subsidiaries which are licensed corporations;                          （財政資源）規則的資本規定；

     •    to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going            •   保障本集團持續經營的能力，
          concern so that it can continue to provide returns for                 以繼續為股東提供回報及為
          shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders;                      其他持份者提供利益；

     •    to support the Group’s stability and growth; and                   •   維持本集團的穩定及發展；及

     •    to maintain a strong capital base to support the development       •   維持穩固的資本基礎，以支持
          of its business.                                                       其業務的發展。

     The liquid capital is monitored daily by each individual licensed       各持牌附屬公司的管理層根據證券
     subsidiary’s management based on the Securities and Futures             及期貨（財政資源）規則每天監控流
     (Financial Resources) Rules. The required information is filed          動 資 金，並 按 時 向 證 監 會 提 呈 所 需
     with the SFC on a timely basis. The SFC requires each licensed          資 料。證 監 會 規 定，各 持 牌 法 團 須
     corporation to maintain a level of required liquid capital which is     保持所需流動資本水平為介乎0.1百
     the higher of the floor requirement ranging from HK$0.1 million to      萬港元至15百萬港元的最低要求及
     HK$15 million and 5% of the aggregate of its adjusted liabilities       其經調整負債及客戶保證金總和的
     and clients’ margin. All licensed corporations within the Group         5%兩 者 中 的 較 高 者。於 截 至2022
     complied with their required liquid capital during the years ended      年 及2021年12月31日 止 年 度，本 集
     31 December 2022 and 2021.                                              團旗下所有持牌法團均符合所需流
